# Stride: every day, every example distance

These are complete outputs from the local Stride engine, generated from the same 15 example inputs as the previous progression report. Only the sample runner and event names have been clarified. They are examples for checking the engine, not your personal profile. All start Monday 21 September 2026.

The input easy planning pace is 6:00/km. These profiles contain no race benchmark or explicit pace target, so the actual saved workouts prescribe effort; no faster pace has been invented. “≈” means a distance estimate for a timed or mixed workout. When a step has kilometres, those are its stopping condition; its time is a planning allowance. Follow steps in their listed order. Rest means no run is scheduled. Race duration is a planning estimate, not a predicted finish time. Weekly training totals exclude race day. Easy-day distances retain the exact generated values, including any small fractions.


---

# 1 km custom: full daily plan

8 weeks, 2026-09-21 to 2026-11-15. Starting input: **20 km/week**, **6 km recent long run**, **4 running days**. Established runner, gradual volume, balanced difficulty, balanced method, Sunday long run, 120-minute weekday limit, 300-minute long-run limit. One weekday quality workout requested, plus the long run.

## Week 1 — Foundation

A shorter easy outing to recover between the week’s key sessions. Keep the effort relaxed.

1. Easy run: 3.667 km (planned 22 min). Conversational · 2–3 / 10

### Tuesday, 2026-09-22

**Rest — no run scheduled.**

### Wednesday, 2026-09-23

**3 × 4 min tempo** · ≈ 5.333 km · 32 min

Accumulate controlled, comfortably hard running with short easy breaks.

1. Warm up: 10 min. Start gently · full sentences · 2–3 / 10
2. Aerobic running before the main set: 2 min. Comfortable aerobic running · 2–3 / 10
3. 4 min tempo · 1 of 3: 4 min. Comfortably hard · 6–7 / 10 · short phrases
4. Easy recovery: 1 min 30 sec. Easy jog; let breathing settle
5. 4 min tempo · 2 of 3: 4 min. Comfortably hard · 6–7 / 10 · short phrases
6. Easy recovery: 1 min 30 sec. Easy jog; let breathing settle
7. 4 min tempo · 3 of 3: 4 min. Comfortably hard · 6–7 / 10 · short phrases
8. Cool down: 5 min. Easy running · finish relaxed

# Marathon: full daily plan

18 weeks, 2026-09-21 to 2027-01-24. Starting input: **70 km/week**, **23 km recent long run**, **5 running days**. Established runner, gradual volume, balanced difficulty, balanced method, Sunday long run, 120-minute weekday limit, 300-minute long-run limit. One weekday quality workout requested, plus the long run.

## Week 1 — Foundation

Training: **70 km** planned total · long **23 km** · 2 quality sessions including long.

28 weeks, 2026-09-21 to 2027-04-04. Starting input: **90 km/week**, **28 km recent long run**, **6 running days**. Established runner, gradual volume, balanced difficulty, balanced method, Sunday long run, 120-minute weekday limit, 240-minute long-run limit. One weekday quality workout requested, plus the long run. Recent ultra evidence: 540 min/week and 168 min longest outing, stable for 16 weeks.

# 100 miles ultra: full daily plan

32 weeks, 2026-09-21 to 2027-05-02. Starting input: **90 km/week**, **28 km recent long run**, **6 running days**. Established runner, gradual volume, balanced difficulty, balanced method, Sunday long run, 120-minute weekday limit, 240-minute long-run limit. One weekday quality workout requested, plus the long run. Recent ultra evidence: 540 min/week and 168 min longest outing, stable for 16 weeks.
